# Test-trace map

Binds every reserved test id (`T-XXXX`, from [`US-TM.md`](US-TM.md)) to its
realization. `kind` is one of:

- `rust` — `crate::test_function` (existence CI-verified)
- `story` — Storybook story with a `play` interaction test (CI-verified)
- `manual` — procedure in [`manual-tests.md`](manual-tests.md) (CI-verified)
- `reserved` — not yet realized; the note says which milestone realizes it

`check-traceability.mjs` fails CI when a US-TM test id is missing here or an
automated reference names a test that does not exist. New ACs land with their
rows in the same PR (see AGENTS.md).

A `rust` reference to an `#[ignore]`d test exists but CI never runs it. The
gate lists every T id with ignored tests, and fails a T id whose every bound
test is ignored unless its note names where and when the test runs: a
`manual:MT-...` procedure or a `ci:<job>` workflow job that runs ignored tests
(`--ignored`). Ids that violated this when the check landed (#1758) sit in a
baseline in the script that may only shrink.

The full H2–H7 acceptance procedures below remain **UNEXECUTED** and their
capabilities remain **PLANNED**. MT-H2-03 separately records the narrower
source-only local-definition assessment. A reference proves that a procedure
exists, not that its AC or delivery gate has passed.

| T-0200 | AC-0200 | rust | app::preflight_defers_eval_proof_and_recovery_reconciles_it, app::a_recovery_that_fails_before_publishing_leaves_preflight_pending, app::a_local_recovery_cancelled_during_reconciliation_writes_no_preflight_findings, app::recovery_fences_only_older_preflights_of_its_repo, ingest::captured_claims_reconcile_against_the_proven_bytes_not_the_live_tree | Real `run_ingest` replaces preflight's register findings with the claim-reconciled scan: proven literals close, const-shaped downgrades out of Unsupported, dynamic stays, and no pending wording survives. A preflight already in flight is refused after recovery (only older runs of the same repo), and the scan reads the captured bytes, including captured files deleted since capture (which still count toward detected languages), so a post-capture edit cannot inherit or drop a claim. The reconciliation scan runs before publication so the coverage inventory is published with its facts (AC-0239), and its write happens only once the job has completed: a cancel landing during the reconciliation scan leaves the pending findings untouched (#489). The summary carries the reconciled report and the store applies it by register order (AC-0216, `ui/src/preflightStore.test.ts`) |

| T-0214 | AC-0214 | rust | app::a_preflight_started_after_a_local_recovery_is_done_keeps_its_findings, app::a_preflight_started_after_a_github_recovery_is_done_keeps_its_findings, app::a_preflight_started_after_a_manifest_recovery_is_done_keeps_its_findings, app::a_reconcile_fence_orders_before_later_preflights | Real `run_ingest`, `add_repo` and `add_system` (#497): a real preflight (`start_preflight`) of every registered source's root, started on the job's `done` event before the reconciliation writes, runs — the managed clone is already ready and unreserved — and its pending findings stay in the register; at the fence level, a reconciliation reserved before a newer preflight persisted writes nothing, one committed before that preflight persists does not refuse it, and a preflight begun before the reservation is still refused afterwards |

| T-0220 | AC-0220 | rust | adapters-lang-ts::literal_bundler_aliases_keep_package_shaped_specifiers_in_system, adapters-lang-ts::a_vite_alias_key_keeps_a_trailing_dollar_literally, adapters-lang-ts::only_an_alias_in_the_exported_config_resolves, adapters-lang-ts::equal_scope_configs_that_disagree_on_an_alias_never_resolve_it | Vite/webpack `resolve.alias` read as data (#463): a package-shaped key with a literal path target (`path.resolve(__dirname, …)`, a relative string) resolves to the real file citing the config's key span; an unproven target (`fileURLToPath(new URL(…))` with no file, an absolute path) stays a Gap naming the config; webpack `key$` is exact while a Vite `key$` is literal; an alias outside the exported config (a nested helper, or one of several alias objects) and same-directory configs that disagree never resolve and stay Gaps; package-to-package and non-literal aliases stay external |
| T-0197 | AC-0197 | rust | app::preflight_command_scans_on_a_worker_and_reports_progress, app::the_report_walk_reports_progress_too, ingest::scan_hook_reports_each_file_and_stops_without_a_partial_report, ingest::scan_hook_reports_the_listing_walk_and_stops_it_mid_tree, source-walk::the_directory_hook_reports_each_directory_and_stops_without_a_partial_list | Preflight (#235) scans on a blocking worker and emits `preflight://progress` with path/done/total, and while listing the tree (#453) pings each directory with the files found so far and a `null` total (story:Ingest/PreflightSurface/ListingFiles); the surface's live line is story:Ingest/PreflightSurface/ScanningWithProgress and the store's progress lifecycle is `ui/src/preflightStore.test.ts`; large-repo interactivity is manual:MT-BB-01 |
| T-0198 | AC-0198 | rust | app::cancelled_preflight_stops_before_the_next_file_and_persists_nothing, app::preflight_cancelled_before_its_worker_starts_never_runs, app::preflight_supersession_follows_invocation_order, app::cancel_never_waits_on_persistence, app::preflight_register_writes_never_interleave, app::cancelled_preflight_stops_while_listing_the_tree, ingest::scan_hook_reports_each_file_and_stops_without_a_partial_report, ingest::scan_hook_reports_the_listing_walk_and_stops_it_mid_tree, source-walk::the_directory_hook_reports_each_directory_and_stops_without_a_partial_list | Cancel/supersede stops before the next file — or, while the tree is still being listed, before the next directory (#453) — and persists no findings; the run registers before its worker is spawned (so an early cancel or a later invocation always wins), register writes never interleave, and Cancel never waits on a write in progress. Progress pings carry their run id, so a superseded scan's ping is ignored. The Cancel control is story:Ingest/PreflightSurface/ScanningWithProgress and story:Ingest/PreflightSurface/Cancelled, and the stale-result guard, cancel-on-switch-to-GitHub and cancel-and-retire-on-path-change (#454) are `ui/src/preflightStore.test.ts` |

recorded limits (ROADMAP W1): each inventory is published in the same transaction as its repo's facts and read back with the same revision. `query_qualified_context` qualifies every registered or recorded repo as recorded, stale (registration, plugin basis or requested layer selection changed, failing closed) or unrecorded. Each repo states what was compared at read time, the block is never exhaustive, it counts inside the byte budget, and cursors bind the snapshot and coverage together. `query_context` is unchanged. Qualified investigation tests cover frozen preparation, bounded inventory acquisition, disclosure, mocked worker execution, and saved result validation. Live-provider acceptance remains a separate gate. UI checks include story:Investigations/SavedCoverage/HistoricalRecordedStaleAndUnrecorded, story:Investigations/SavedCoverage/NoRecordedLimitationIsNotCompleteSupport and the actual backend DTO fixture in `ui/src/investigationStore.test.ts`. #642 verifies shared immutable projection ownership and measured construction work against the prior two-projection baseline. |

Retry after an injected failure completes the reclaim. The bounded ledger prunes oldest-first to unknown. ### Decision #1688 capacity-refusal regression coverage

- `agents::capacity_exclusion_preserves_legacy_menu_and_all_refused_obligations`:
  cumulative exclusions, exhausted acquisition menu, unchanged obligation state,
  and empty-exclusion legacy bytes.
- `agents::capacity_exhausted_analyst21_seals_finish_only_without_action_refusal`:
  version-bound finish-only admission without a fabricated action refusal.
- `app::capacity_exclusion_durable_replay_preserves_analyst20_and_excludes_for_21`:
  durable capacity refusal, historical cutoff, revision isolation, and subsequent
  call sealing/replay for both analyst versions.
- `app::captured_receipt_family_preserves_history_and_binds_acquisition_identity`:
  distinct @21 receipt family, preserved historical families, and rejected role
  mismatches. These fixture checks do not establish real-model acceptance.

- `app::analyst21_durable_capacity_refusals_exclude_two_distinct_reads`:
  two distinct refused source reads remain excluded together after durable reopen,
  then return after successful inventory publication advances the revision while
  historical refusal charges remain;
  this supplements the single-action exhaustion and historical-version test.
- `app::run10_model_response_and_capacity_menu_match_saved_action_hash`:
  independently parsed run-10 model response and typed offered action both match
  the saved `d92e64c2…` hash through the production response and menu hash paths.

## Decision #1692 authenticated diagnostics (AC-0287)

- `agents::replay_location_spans_fields_without_echoing_text`: intentional
  cross-field detection produces typed endpoints without copied text.
- `agents::replay_location_tracks_sentence_and_preserves_metadata_and_sensitive_verdicts`:
  source/metadata distinction, sentence position and sensitive-output refusal.
- `agents::replay_location_reports_unicode_and_sentence_ranges`: Unicode
  scalar ranges, whitespace boundaries and oversized prose rejection.
- `agents::replay_location_rejects_reversed_and_out_of_shape_positions`:
  shape/order checks; these do not authenticate saved diagnostic correctness.
- `agents::correction_prose_is_checked_even_with_unadmitted_citations`:
  input adapter preserves rejection and never admits invalid citations.
- `agents::capacity_exclusion_preserves_legacy_menu_and_all_refused_obligations`
  and `agents::obligation_acquisition_choices_reject_foreign_state_and_suppress_empty_discovery`:
  separate capacity IDs, unrelated blocked targets, legacy menu equality and reset.

No provider execution or release acceptance is claimed by these fixtures.
Admission checks locator correctness against transient text; reopen checks only
integrity and invocation binding after that text has been discarded.

- `agents::analyst20_and_21_foundations_preserve_menu_and_feedback_wire`: independent
  historical menu field layout and exact shared replay-feedback JSON bytes.

- Native `analyst22_replay_locator_is_bound_and_survives_reopen_without_text`:
  schema-4 typed location, no copied metadata in feedback, independent response
  witness and authenticated durable reopen.
- Native `analyst21_durable_capacity_refusals_exclude_two_distinct_reads`:
  @21/@22 exact-hash exclusions across reopen; @22 feedback separates excluded
  obligations without marking attempts/acquisition, and ledger advancement clears
  the private exclusion projection while preserving historical charges.
- Native `analyst22_latest_refusal_requires_independent_feedback_witness`:
  missing/changed digest in both journal and response, rewritten feedback with
  recomputed identities, and missing independent response all fail closed even
  before another invocation exists; transactional mutations are rolled back.

- `agents::analyst22_locator_discrepancy_preserves_refusal_and_real_errors`:
  injected missing/disagreeing diagnostics retain a refused finish with generic
  schema-3 feedback; sensitive-output, size and invalid-action failures propagate.

## Decision #1697 claim diagnostics (AC-0287)

- `agents::all_r3_goldens_against_exact_cited_spans`: all nine retained r3
  findings and exact captured source ranges; false universal/default claims are
  refused while the accurate findings and finding-5 body remain admitted by the
  checker. This establishes checker behavior, not actual-model acceptance.
- `agents::analyst23_claim_feedback_is_versioned_and_text_free`: schema-5
  enumeration/configuration reasons contain only a typed sentence location;
  historical schemas and text-bearing locator objects cannot substitute for it.
- Native `analyst23_latest_refusal_requires_independent_feedback_witness`:
  the @23 path rejects missing/rewritten independent response witnesses using
  the same durable checks as @22. This fixture exercises citation correction;
  claim-specific coverage is provided by the captured-source test below.
- Native `citation_correction_is_once_only_after_reopen_and_duplicate_publication`:
  includes @23 and retains the single correction allowance through reopen.

- Native `analyst23_configuration_claim_refusal_survives_captured_source_reopen`:
  real retained-source reservation/read/publication, policy-bound invocation and
  normal finish admission produce schema-5 configuration feedback. Independent
  reopen verifies the title/sentence locator and text-free feedback; missing or
  changed witness, rewritten locator with recomputed journal hashes, and deleted
  response are rejected in rolled-back test transactions.
- `agents::single_word_configuration_subject_is_not_applicable`: pins the
  delegated multiword-only scope; Redis in cited source does not bind a subject.

### Decision #1701 — AC-0287 reservation and combined feedback

Initial serialization primitive coverage (integration remains pending):

- `rust:adapters-lang-ts::captured::receipt::sizes::tests::measured_receipt_roundtrips_and_compacts_without_changing_legacy`

  exercises measured capture, persisted receipts, graph binding, charged read and
